[\[88a\]]
[\[88a.1\]]go cha bgo bar bya'o/_/de kho na nyid kyi bde ba chen po'i mchod pa'i mthar gsang sngags kyi phreng ba bzlas pa'i rnal 'byor la gnas pas so/_/de rjes la thun nye bar bsdu bas 'gro ba sbyang ba'i bsam gtan dang ldan pas rim gyis dkyil 'khor yongs su bsdus te/_bdud 
[\[88a.2\]]rtsi myang ba dang gtor ma dbul bar bya/_des ni mnyam gzhag gi rnal 'byor ro/_/mnyam langs rjes thob la thun mtshams kyi bya ba spyod lam la 'jug par bya'o/_/rdzogs pa'i rim pa lam du bya ba ni/_de nas 'bar ba'i me la ni/_/sreg blugs dgu ni sbyin par bya/_/'bar phreng rgya mtshor 
[\[88a.3\]]blta bar bya/_/kun nas sgo yi 'khor lor nges/_/zhes pa don gyis bde stong lhan cig skyes pa'i ye shes nyid la gnas pas/_phyag rgya chen po bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i don la gnas pas/_dus dang rnam pa thams cad du dpal he ru ka 'khor lo sdom pa'i nga rgyal du gnas par bya ba nyid 
[\[88a.4\]]do/_/'di ni he ru ka mngal skyes nyams su blang ba'i rim pa dpal DoM bi he ru ka'i dgongs pa'o/_/de la dpal he ru ka mngal skyes kyi bskyed pa'i rim pa'i khyad par bstan pa'i gzhung bsdu ba bstan pa/_de nas rtag tu sgrub pa pos zhes rnal 'byor pas thun bzhi la sogs par nyams su blang 
[\[88a.5\]]ba'o/_/gsang sngags ni rtsa ba'i dang snying po dang nye snying la sogs so/_/lha ni shes nas sgom pa bsnyen sgrub bzhis sam sbyor ba dang po la sogs gsum gyis nyams su blang pa mnyam rjes 'brel pas ma yengs par bsam gtan la gnas pa'o/_/de nyid yon tan ni kun brtags 'khrul 
[\[88a.6\]]ba bzlog pa brtan g.yo gzhal yas khang dang lhar 'char ba'o/_/gzhan dbang las dang nyon mongs pa rnam par dag pas ye shes su 'char/_yongs grub 'khor 'das kyi chos nyid ro mnyam lhan cig skyes pa'i ye shes su 'char ba'i khyad par gyis/_zhi gnas lhag 
[\[88a.7\]]mthong dbyer med du 'gyur bas byang chub sems bde chen rnam par mi rtog pa sgrub pa pos ni 'dod 'grub 'gyur zhes de bzhin gshegs pa'i phrin las la dbang bsgyur ba'o/_/le'u'i mtshan ni ro langs sgrub pa zhes dur khrod kyi zhing skyong dang sha za sogs bstim ste las kyi pho nya 
[\[88b\]]
[\[88b.1\]]ma bskul zhing bsgrub pa'o/_/bskyed pa'i rim pa ni skye gnas kyi gzhi'i gnyen pos sbyor byed mngon par byang chub pa'i cho ga'i le'u ste sum cu rtsa gnyis pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//de nas dpal ldan mchog gi spyod pa gsang bar bstan pas bsnyen dang nye bsnyen sgrub dang sgrub chen ldan/_/
[\[88b.2\]]sbyin sreg pho nya legs bstan ma zhen thabs bcas bde chen mchod bya phrin las 'grub/_/da ni sa ma ya na ya ga Na mu pU dza zhes pas dam tshig tshul la tshogs kyi mchod pa bstan/_/de nas sgrub pa po rnams kyis zhes smos te/_rim pa gnyis la gnas pa'i gang zag dam tshig dang 
[\[88b.3\]]sdom pa ldan zhing spyod pa a wa d+hU tI pa sbas pa la spyod pa gsang ba can no/_/sbyor ba ni rigs ldan ma lhar bskyed pas bde chen du ste/_kun la med pa bshad ni shin tu gsang ba ste/_b+hu su ku'i spyod pa ltar byas pas rab tu dben pa'i tshul gyis spros bcas spros med shin tu spros med kyi 
[\[88b.4\]]tshogs kyi 'khor lo dang 'brel ba'o/_/sbyor ba de yang khyad par du ni 'di lta ste/_gnas phun sum tshogs par dus khyad par can tshes bcu la sogs pa la lha grangs dang mnyam pa'am/_de ma yin yang dkyil 'khor 'khor lo'i bsam gtan la gnas pa tshogs pa brda byas pas/_
[\[88b.5\]]khro bo sngon pos bgegs bskrad cing khrus byas nas nang du 'ongs pa la rnam par bkod pa'i dkyil 'khor ram/_tshogs kyi slob dpon gyis lha'i 'khor lo la mchod bstod byas nas/_der lha'i ting nge 'dzin dang ldan pa'i rnal 'byor pa dang /_rigs ldan ma la sku gsung thugs byin gyis 
[\[88b.6\]]brlab cing bzlas brjod byas la/_mchod pa la byug pa sngon du 'gro bas/_tshogs rdzas dang gtor ma byin gyis brlabs/_lag mchod dang 'brel bas mchod pa ni rig ma bcu drug la sogs pa dbul/_de nas rgyan la sogs pa dbul te/_oM badzra mu ku Ta/_oM kuN+Da li pU dza/_kaN+Thaka_/
[\[88b.7\]]ke yU ra/_ang+gu li/_me kha la/_nU pu ra/_ha ra/_mar+d+ha ha ra/_pA dka_/puSh+pa ma la pU dza/_oM tsan+d+ra/_srur+ya/_bi tA na/_d+h+wa dza/_puSh+pa ka ri Ni/_oM pu ri twa ra Ni/_tsa ma ra/_d+harma tA tsakra/_tshat+t+ra/_/A daR+sha/_pakac+cha/_tsakra/_tsin+tA ma Ni/_man+d+ri/_si ka pu ru Sha rat+na/_ha sti rat+na/_a shwa rat+na/_khaD+ga rat+na/_oM shaM kha b+hA 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]Sha/_tsakra/_shrI bat+sa/_tshan+t+ra rat+na/_man+dA ra/_d+h+wa dza/_mat+sa/_ka la sha pU dza me g+ha sa mu dra spa ra Na sa ma ya hUM zhes pa/_mchod pa sna tshogs rol mo'i bye brag longs spyod kyis mchod/_de nas thams cad kyang lha'i nga rgyal dang ldan pas lus kyi dkyil 'khor gsal btab la/_
[\[89a.2\]]kye rdo rje'i sbyor bas nang gi sbyin sreg lam du bya bas dang po bla ma dang lha'i 'khor lo mkha' 'gro'i tshogs la mchod cing /_so sor rtog pa dral ba'i cho gas sha lnga bdud rtsi lnga la longs spyad par bya ba'o/_/mthar glu dang gar dang phyi nang bde ba chen po de kho na nyid rnal 'byor gyi ye shes 
[\[89a.3\]]gsang ba lhan cig skyes pa'i nyams bskyang bas kun nas dang bas ci nus par mchod par bya ba/_lhag gtor phyir gtang bar bya zhing /_mthar bsngo zhing smon lam gdab par bya bas yi ge brgya pa brjod par bya'o/_/des ni dngos grub thams cad nges par 'grub par 'gyur ba ste le'u'i 
[\[89a.4\]]mtshan ni gsang ba'i mchod pa lha'i tshogs phyi nang kun du bde ba chen pos so/_/bsnyen bkur gyi cho ga ni tshogs kyi 'khor lo ste le'u sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//khyad par yang dag ye shes mkha' 'gro dra ba sdom pa rgya mtsho chen po'i 'khor lo yi/_/dkyil 'khor rim 
[\[89a.5\]]pa bsdus las dpal ldan rdo rje rnal 'byor ma yis gsang sngags rigs pa rgyu skyes ldan/_/'dir ni gnas skabs dang mthar thug gi dngos grub thams cad myur du sgrub par byed pa bstan pa ni/_de nas zhes smos te/_mkha' 'gro ma ni dpal rdo rje rnal 'byor ma sku gsung thugs rdo rje'i don 
[\[89a.6\]]kun sgrub par byed pa'i tshogs kyi dra ba sdom pa rgya mtsho'i dgongs pa'o/_/dpal 'khor lo sdom pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi lha tshogs thams cad kyi cho ga rgyas pa ste zab pa dang rgya che ba'o/_/las gang la ni kun brjod pa ni phyi nang de kho na nyid ye shes kyi sbyin sreg las/_zhi ba dang rgyas pa dbang 
[\[89a.7\]]dgug dang mngon spyod la sogs pa las chen rnams dang dngos grub 'grub par the tshom med pa'o zhes pas rdo rje phag mo zhal gcig phyag pa zhi mas phrin las myur du sgrub pa nyid do/_/de la 'dir sbyin sreg bstan pa las phyi'i thab khung dang rdzas la sogs pa me lha mchod pa la sogs 
[\[89b\]]
[\[89b.1\]]pa yang gzung ngo /_/yang lha'i rnal 'byor dang sbyor ba ni/_mkha' 'gro rgya mtsho las/_dpal ldan dpa' bo he ru ka_/de yi yan lag 'khor lor gnas/_/rnal 'byor ma ni sa gsum spyod/_/a haM shes rab thabs bdag nyid/_/lha yi rnal 'byor snying ga ste/_/gong ma ga bur sih+la las/_/
[\[89b.2\]]ro rkyang rtsa dang p+hU tIr/_srog rtsol bcas pa'i sbyor ba'o/_/bsam gtan gnas pas mngon byang chub/_/sa bon rdo rje lnga bdag nyid/_/shes pa dag byed sku rdzogs bya/_/lte ba sprul par pad+ma la/_/A li kA li nges par sbyar/_/a wa d+hU tIr sems dpa' che/_/'bar 'dzag bdud rtsi 
[\[89b.3\]]thig le ni/_/mdzod spu'i gnas las nges par gzhug_/dpa' bo nus pa bzlog gyur pas/_/zhal gyi thigs pa byas chags 'bar/_/tsaN+Da lI yis bsregs de nas/_/bde ba 'bar ba me las ni/_/bsreg bya phung po 'byung bdud rtsi/_/drag 'bar thig le bcu drug gi/_/phreng ba ye shes rgya mtsho che/_/'od 'phro kun 
[\[89b.4\]]nas lus kyi sgo/_/mgal me'i 'khor lo ltar 'khor gyur/_/dbang phyug dpa' bo dpyid rgyas pas/_/zhal bzhi de nyid phyag rgya che/_/thig les mnyam gnas dga' ba che/_/lhan cig skyes pa mtshan nyid bral/_/skad cig sku gsung thugs ye shes/_/rdo rje snying po rtsa pad+ma/_/'dab 
[\[89b.5\]]gnas yi ge dkar dmar gsal/_/'byung po'i pho nya rtags dga' bzhi/_/gnas 'dul dpal ldan 'khor los sgyur/_/dpa' bo rnal 'byor gnyis med bde/_/phyi rol 'khrul 'khor las rlung dgug_/khaN+Da ro has skul byed yin/_/yul dang dbang po mngon zhen pa/_/ye shes 
[\[89b.6\]]ro mnyam rang gi lus/_/kun 'dar 'khor lo kun dbus skyes/_/dus dang chu bo'i 'khor lo ni/_/gsang ba rnal 'byor lus gnas yin/_/zhes gsang ba bde ba chen po'i de kho na nyid bstan pa'o/_/le'u'i mtshan rnam par bshad pa ni pho nya mo'i sbyin sreg ni phyi nang gsang ba de kho na nyid

[\[90a\]]
[\[90a.1\]]gzhan bdud rtsi len pa'i dus rtags phyogs dang yon tan gdengs can mthar/_/sku gsung thugs kyi sgo rnams mngon 'gyur dus min 'chi bdag 'joms par 'gyur bas dpa' dbang bde ba thob/_/de la phyi nang de kho na nyid gsang ba'i don bla na med pa bstan pa'i phyir/_de nas

[\[90a.3\]]stong du shar ba'o/_/ji bzhin rim gyis mtshon pa ste ni phrin las thams cad pa'i bye brag dang /_gtum mo 'bar ba las thig le bzhu btul dang /_snyoms par 'jug pa'i bde ba lhan cig skyes pa nyid lam du bya ba snang grags bde stong du 'char ba'i don to/_/mchod pa gnyis med sbyor bdag

[\[90a.5\]]gnyis su med pa ro mnyam du ston pa'o/_/kha sbyar bar gyur pa dang /_rdo rje lu gu rgyud bcings pa/_/zhes pa la sogs pa'i don ni dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las/_thabs dang shes rab mnyam sbyor bas/_/kha sbyor zhes byar brjod pa yin/_/rtsa gnyis kun du 'khyud pa ni/_/rdo rje lu gu rgyud

[\[90a.7\]]zhing brjod/_/de nas chags sogs nyon mongs rnams/_/dbug la dum bu mda' sbyar bya/_/shin tu rgya che dmigs pa ste/_/kun rdzob btung ba mchog nyid dang /_/dngos kun rdo rjes rnam dag bya/_/rdo rje zhes bya de bzhin gshegs/_/ji ltar rgyal ba de dbus su/_/rtag tu 'byin dang sdud 
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]pa'o/_/yi ge pho nya la sogs sngags/_/mda' yi shugs bzhin yongs su 'khor/_/sprul pa'i 'khor lo'i dbus su ni /sde tshan brgyad kyis shin tu bskor/_/sngags rnams thams cad 'byung gnas te/_/phan tshun ci yang rung ba bskyed/_/chos kyi 'khor lo zhes bya ba/_/lus can

[\[90b.3\]]gnyis ldan/_/zhes bya ba yang de nyid do/_/sa bon phreng ba de nas bsgom/_/hUM gi skud pas brgyu bar bya/_/bsgrub bya 'gog par gyur pa ni/_/gom pa gcig kyang mi 'gro'o/_/'di ni shes rab lhan skyes te/_/de yi gzugs su gyur pas na/_/las kyi rlung gis bskyod nas su/_/lte ba'i

[\[90b.6\]]dpa' thams cad kyi/_/shangs sgo g.yon du zhugs nas ni/_/spyi bo'i 'khor lo bsregs nas su/_/bdud rtsi ye shes bcud bsdus pa/_/sngon gyi lam nas slar byung ste/_/slar yang rnal 'byor spyi gtsug zhugs/_/bsreg par gyur pa'i sangs rgyas rnams/_/bde chen rgyas pa'i lte ba 
[\[90b.7\]]yi/_/dkyil 'khor du ni son gyur nas/_/sngon bzhin du ni gnas par 'gyur/_/zhes gsungs pa'i don to/_/rang rang phyogs kyis phye ba yin/_/'jug pa yis ni dbye ba nyid/_/ces pa'i don la/_zla ba dang ni nyi ma dag_/'og dang steng gi sgo gnyis te/_/

[\[91a.1\]]gzhad 'byed pa ni gser sgo ste/_/dzA lan+darar gtugs pas bzlog_/phyir 'gro nang du 'ong bas so/_/'gog pa'i dus dang dpyid kyi dus/_/ji srid g.yas dang g.yon pa yi/_/rlung dang yi ge thig le 'gog_/kun 'dar 'gram gyi rtsa dang rtse/_/dzA lan+d+ha 
[\[91a.2\]]rar 'gog 'gyur ba'o/_/skye ba rab tu 'phel ba'i dus/_/sdud pa 'grib cing ldog pa'i dus/_/gnas pa mnyam par 'gyur ba ste/_/rab tu thim pas stong par gyur/_/tsha grang 'byung ba'i khyad par phyed/_/nyin mo 'phel ba byang bgrod dus/_/mtshan mo 'phel ba lhor bgrod dus/_/mnyam 
[\[91a.3\]]pa dus ldan gnas pa'o/_/g.yas nas 'phel ba g.yon rtser ldog_/g.yon nas 'phel ba g.yas rtser ldog_/drod kyang lugs 'byung lugs bzlog las/_/brtan pa dang ni g.yo ba las/_/drod ni 'phel 'grib can yin te/_/gyen du bsnyag dang thur du 'jug_/'chi bdag las ni

[\[91a.5\]]ba slu ba'i cho ga'i le'u ste sum cu rtsa lnga pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//gsang ba de kho na nyid mchod pa rig ma dang ni gnyis med bde ba lam du bya ba nyid/_/rim dang rim pa med pas dpyid dang thig le sos dang ston dus kyis phye zla ba ngos bzung ba'o/_/'dir dpal 'khor lo bde mchog gi 
[\[91a.6\]]rnal 'byor pas/_lus sems snang ba bde ba lam du bya ba'i phyir/_de nas phyag rgya'i mchod chen dang /_/zhes smos te/_'di nyid las/_le'u dang po las/_bsrub bya srub byed yang dag sbyor/_/zhes pa dang /_sbyor nyid cho ga'i ye shes zhes pa rgyas par bshad pa/_'di ni dkyil 'khor 'khor 
[\[91a.7\]]lo'i lam bla na med pa bstan pa nyid do/_/de la rnal 'byor rigs ldan dang /_/thun mong sngags kyi dgod pa mchog_/go cha chen po bgo ba dang /_/snying po nye snying lte ba dang /_/snying ga mgrin pa dpral bar ni/_/nyi zla gnas la gsal ba blta/_/phyag rgya snying po mchod pa 'dis/_/bde 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]chen gsang ba grub pa nyid/_/myur 'grub yin par the tshom med/_/blo ldan dpa' bo lha mchog gis/_/rigs ldan rnal 'byor ma bsrub bya/_/lhar gsal bde bar snyoms zhugs pas/_/haM las bdud rtsi byang chub sems/_/rab 'bab bcu drug cha bcas pa/_/de nyid kun 'dar

[\[91b.3\]]bde ba gsang ba yi/_/thig le snying po gsang bar gnas/_/gnyis shor gnyis mnyam dus su ni/_/lhan cig skyes pa'i dga' ba ste/_/mtshan nyid bral ba'i skad cig ma/_/rab rdzogs dga' bral rnam par nyid/_/ro mnyam rdo rje mi 'gyur ba'o/_/de nyid chags pa rnam dag pa/_/
[\[91b.4\]]dri med lus ngag sems dag ni/_/'dam skyes pad+ma ltar gsal 'gyur/_/phyag rgya grub pa'i lus rnams ni/_/bgrod pas 'dod yon lam du 'gyur/_/sa bon 'od 'bar mtshon cha drag_/sngags ldan lha mo la sbyar ba/_/snying po gsang ba cho ga nyid/_/'bad pa rgyu 'bras rgyas 
[\[91b.5\]]gdab 'gyur/_/'bras bu rnams dbye skad cig ma/_/phyin ci ma log gsang ba las/_/thugs ka nas phyung nges par spro/_/rnam shes tshar bcad ye shes kyis/_/phan byed dngos 'dzin las dag 'gyur/_/thabs shes dbye ba 'khor lo yi/_/rtsa yi lam mchog rdo rje yis/_/

[\[91b.7\]]pa'i cho ga ni bde ba lam du gyur pa lugs su 'byung ba dang /_lugs las bzlog pa'i rim pa'i skye tshul/_des dbang phyug brgyad ldan gyi gsang ba bstan pa'i le'u ste sum cu rtsa drug pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//bde chen sdom pa lam du byed pas spyod pa grub pas rigs ldan 
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]thams cad sa gsum por ni dbang du byed/_/gang du dgug byed smin bsreg sbas pa'i lam nyid dam tshig ldan pas nges 'grub 'gyur/_/'dir rigs ldan gyi khyad par gyi mtshan nyid spyod pa yongs su shes pas dbang du bya ba dang /_de kho na nyid kyi mchod pa'i 
[\[92a.2\]]gsang ba bstan pa la/_rnal 'byor pa la rjes su chags par 'gyur ba med pa ni/_brtan par bya ba la dbang thob par mi 'gyur bas/_de nyid nges par bya ba ni/_de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba ni pho nya'i lam pas ni rigs ldan dbang du bya ba la brten 'dod yon lam du khyer bas phyag rgya chen 
[\[92a.3\]]po 'thob pa dang /_rnal 'byor gyi lam pas/_de kho na nyid sbas pa'i don gyis rang lus thabs lam du gyur pas ye shes grub pa bla na med pa bstan pas/_phrin las thams cad nang gsang ba'i rigs ldan gyis grub par bstan pa'o/_/tshar bcad grub pa'i cho ga ste/_/zhes pa/_bdag la mngon 
[\[92a.4\]]par mi phyogs pa la/_rdzas dang 'khrul 'khor gyi sbyor ba dang /_sngags dang bsam gtan nus pa drag tu dmigs pas so/_/rjes su 'dzin pa ni sngags dang rdzas tsam gyis 'grub pa'o/_/grol lam pas cho ga ni de kho na nyid lnga ldan gyi rim pa ste/_bdag gi de kho na nyid ni rnal 'byor pa'i lus 
[\[92a.5\]]sku gsum las byung ba'i rtsa yi ge thig le rlung gi phra rags spyi dang bye brag rtogs pa'o/_/lha'i de kho na nyid rang lus gdod ma nas dkyil 'khor yongs rdzogs su shes pas so/_/sngags kyi de kho na nyid ni yi ge'i rtsa ba e waM las grub tshul bstan pa phyi mo brgyad dang A li kA li'i tshul lo/_/

[\[92b.1\]]dpal rdo rje rnal 'byor ma nyid de de dang gcig tu sbyor ba dpal he ru ka ste/_yab yum thabs shes rab dbyings rig dbyer med/_rigs kyi dkyil 'khor thams cad spro ba dang bsdu ba'i dbang phyug phun sum tshogs pa gsang ba mi 'gyur ba'o/_/dbang du byed pa la tshogs kyi zhal zas sbyar ba'i bye

[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]pa'i rnam par bshad pa'o//_//'dir ni dngos grub nye ba'i rtags cung zad 'byung ba la/_le'u dang po las/_mchog dang 'bring dang tha ma ste/_/pho nyas lhan cig skyes dngos grub/_/ces pa'i don bstan pa'i phyir/_de nas sgrub pos rnal 'byor che/_/zhes smos te/_rnal 'byor te

[\[93b.1\]]spyod pa gsang ba thabs shes rab lha'i nga rgyal gyis snyom par 'jug pa'i tshe/_bzhad pa bzhi ni dga' ba bzhi nyams su myong ba las/_byang sems gyen du bzlog pa lus kyi 'khrul 'khor rlung gnad sngags kyi rnam pa 'dren pa'i yi ge rtsa gnas su dgram par bya ba brgyad du dgod ces pa

[\[117b.7\]]'jigs byed sum bltab tu rnam par gnon cing ste chad pa dang rtag par lta ba 'joms pa'o/_/bcom ldan 'das kyi rtsa ba'i phyag dang po gnyis kyis rdo rje dang dril bu 'dzin pas yum rdo rje rnal 'byor ma'i sku la 'khyud cing mnyam sbyor ba'o/_/nyi shu rtsa bzhi phyed ldan pa na phyag bcu 
[\[118a\]]
[\[118a.1\]]gnyis rten 'brel bcu gnyis dang sa bcu gnyis rdzogs pas bcu gsum rdo rje 'dzin pa la gnas pa'o/_/'ga' zhig gis lha bcu gsum pa'i tshig zin du 'dod pa'ang yod do/_/dpa' bo ni sku gsung thugs kyi 'khor lo la gnas pa'i tshogs so/_/rnal 'byor ma 
[\[118a.2\]]tshogs DA ki ma sum cu rtsa drug gis yongs su bskor ba'o/_/de rnams kyi sku'i rgyan ni/_spyi gtsug gi 'khor lo thod pa lnga nor bu dang ldan pa mi bskyod pa'i phyag rgya dang /_mgul rgyan rin chen 'byung ldan gyi phyag rgya/_lag pa gdu bu dpung rgyan de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs kyi phyag 
[\[118a.3\]]rgya/_rna rgyan 'od dpag med kyi phyag rgya/_tshangs pa'i skud pa mchod phyir thogs dang ska rags 'og pags sil sil sgra sgrogs pa ni dam tshig rigs kyi phyag rgya'o/_/thal ba'i rdul gyis byugs pa ni dpal rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgya nyid do/_/do shal mgo bo'i phreng ba ni mi mgo 
[\[118a.4\]]khrag 'dzag pa can lnga bcu thag pa rgyu mas byas pa 'du byed lnga bcu dag pa'i brda'o/_/shI he ru ka phyag rgya chen po ni/_bcom ldan 'khor lo lnga'i dbus na sku lnga bde chen du bzhugs pa'o/_/go 'phang mchog ni bsgom par bya/_/zhes pas dngos grub thams cad sgrub par 
[\[118a.5\]]'dod pas thun bzhi rim pas gnas par bya ba'o/_/da ni mngal skyes ltar na khyad par ni dpa' bo nyer bzhi po kun dang rnal 'byor ma sum cu so drug pa'i 'khor lo lnga ldan du gyur pa b+ha ga'i dkyil 'khor gyi dbus su dpal he ru ka yab yum sbyor ldan bsrubs bas pa las byung ba'i mngon par 
[\[118a.6\]]byang chub pa lnga las byung ba rgyu'i he ru ka rdzogs pa'o/_/'byin pa dam tshig gi dkyil 'khor du bkod pas/_sdud pa gtso bo yab yum zhu ba ni stong nyid 'od gsal du 'jug par mdzad pa yin zhes thig le'i gzugs su gnas pa la/_mtshams kyi lha mo'i glus bskul bas lha tshogs 
[\[118a.7\]]thams cad dgyes pas khyab par gyur cing gnyis med 'gyur ba 'bras bu'i he ru ka slar bzhengs pa nyid cho ga bla na med pa ting nge 'dzin gsum du bstan pa'o/_/de las bcom ldan 'das kyis gdul bya 'gro ba rnams kyi mos pa'i bye brag dang dbang po'i bye brag tha dad pa du ma'i sems can la sku'i rnam 
[\[118b\]]
[\[118b.1\]]par 'phrul ba gang la gang 'dul du ston pa'o/_/spyod pa sna tshogs ston mdzad de ni zhi ba dang khro bo dang nyan rang dang bo d+hi satwa rnal 'byor dbang phyug dang zhal gdong brnyan mi 'dra bar gar gyi rol pas sna tshogs tshul gyis thun mong dang khyad par gyis 'khor ba ma stongs bar du gdul bya nam mkha' 
[\[118b.2\]]dang mnyam pa la thabs ni sna tshogs sgyu ma lta bu'i spros pa dang /_yang spros pa med pa zab pa dang /_rgya che ba du ma ston pa yin zhes snod dang 'tsham par ro/_/da ni gsang chen bla na med pa zab mo snying po'i chos 'di ni he ru ka ngas ston pa la 'ga' zhig gal te/_mos par ma gyur kyang zhes dbang po dman 
[\[118b.3\]]pas 'di la mi 'jug na yang smod pa dag tu mi bya ste/_tshar bcad pa'i spyod yul lo/_/chos nyid bsam mi khyab dran bya ni thams cad la blo'i yul las 'das pa phyag rgya chen po yid la mi byed pa la gnas par bya ba'o/_/yang gang zag gang gis chos 'di bdag gis rtogs pa'i spyod yul 
[\[118b.4\]]min pas sus kyang 'di'i don mngon du gyur pa med pa'i shes byed ni/_bdag gis chos 'di'i chos nyid ni ma rtogs pas gzhan dag kyang ma shes pa yin no zhes zer ba can ni ma rtogs pa ste/_gang gis mngon du byed pa bstan pa rdzogs sangs rgyas dang zhes dbang po yang rab dang de'i 
[\[118b.5\]]sras ni byang chub sems dpa' dbang po rab dang 'bring du gyur pa rnams dang bdag nyid chen po gang zag thod rgal ba rnal 'byor dbang phyug tu gyur pa rnams kyis mkhyen cing mngon du gyur par mdzad pa'o/_/de la chos thams cad kyi chos nyid lhun grub 'gyur ba med par ston pa'i phyir/_sangs 
[\[118b.6\]]rgyas rnams ni byon gyur tam/_/zhes 'jig rten du de bzhin gshegs pa dpag tu med pa byon nas chos dpag tu med pa gsungs pa'am/_ma byon na yang chos mang po ma gsungs na yang dus dang rnam pa kun du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku gsung thugs ye shes bde ba chen po 
[\[118b.7\]]bsam gyis mi khyab pa dpal bde mchog gi 'khor lo'i chos nyid kyi ngo bo nyid dbyings ye shes dbyer med la ni 'phel 'grib spangs pa ste zung 'jug rab tu mi gnas pa brjod pa las 'das pa nyid do/_/de ltar chos ni tshig dang don du bcas pa/_brjod bya'i gsang ba dang rjod byed kyi 
[\[119a\]]
[\[119a.1\]]gsang chen 'di la lung dang rigs pas rnam par gzhal nas kyang zhes gang zag dbang po rnon po la dgongs nas gsang chen byin rlabs gsang ba mchog tu gyur pa bstan pa la/_'jig rten pa tha snyad pa dngos po la mtshan mar 'dzin pa kun gyis yong mi smad de/_sgro 'dogs 
[\[119a.2\]]skur 'debs kyi spros pa las 'das pa'i phyir ro/_/khyad par snying po gsang ba de'i bgrod pa lam myur zhing bde ba'i lam mchog 'dir bsams shing zhes chos sku zab pa dbyings ye shes ro mnyam pa'i ngang las/_rgya che ba sangs rgyas gzugs sku'i rnam par rol pa gsang ba bsam gyis 
[\[119a.3\]]mi khyab cing /_sku gsung thugs mi zad pa rgyan gyi 'khor lo thams cad mkhyen pa/_ngo bo nyid bde ba chen po byang chub sems lhun gyis grub pa'o/_/sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa la 'jug pa bstan pa ni/_mdo sde ni mtshan nyid kyi theg pa chung ngu dang theg pa chen po rnams so/_/ji srid bya ba spyod 
[\[119a.4\]]sogs kr-i ya dang tsar+ya spyod pa'i rgyud dang rnal 'byor gyi bar rdo rje theg pa spyi'o/_/khyad par ba rnal 'byor gsang mtha' ste bla na med pa dbye ba nyid ni rgyud sde bzhi rim pas 'jug pa la sems can mos pas 'jug pa rnams blo'i rim pa'am/_'dod pa'i yon tan la chags tshul rim pas bstan 
[\[119a.5\]]pa de dang /_de la dga' ba bzhis zhes drang ba dang nges pa dang snying po'i don bstan pa'o/_/mtshan gyis rab grol dbang phyug bde chen go 'phang yan lag mngon 'gyur mi phyed gnas thob pa/_/gang zag u dum ltar te phyir ldog gang yin 'khor bar lhung la phyag rgyar grub pas lam gyur pa/_/
[\[119a.6\]]skad cig dag gis rdzogs byed rnal 'byor de nyid gsang ba srid pa skye tshul dang mtshungs ston/_/'gro ba skal ldan skal med 'jig rten dag na chos rnams dag cing bdag med mnyam pa nyid du rang 'byung ba'o/_/nyan rang theg chen zhugs dang bram zer spyod dang brtul zhugs can de rnal 'byor 
[\[119a.7\]]rjes su rigs pa dang /_/mnyam sbyor mi 'gyur de nyid thams cad sems kyi spyod yul gsang ba mi zad chen por brtag_/gang du stong dang snying rje dbyer med chos nyid bden pa'i don ni mi rtogs byin rlabs bral/_/tshul ngan rig byed rtog ge'i gzhung lugs log par lta la zhen byas rig 
[\[119b\]]
[\[119b.1\]]don ma mthong /_/bla ma'i man ngag bral ba'i skye bo rtog pa'i dra bas bcings pa rnams kyis rtogs min pa/_gsang chen snying po'i rgyud ni dri med 'phags pa'i nor gyi blo ldan spyod yul dag tu ni/_/log lta nyams byed skal bar ldan rnams yang dag lam ston mkhas rnams bsrung /_/mi bzed snyigs 
[\[119b.2\]]dus rtsod pa'i 'gro rnams gsang chen lam rtogs bya phyir nga nyid de yi tshe na yang /_/dus dang dus su snying po'i don ston bla ma nyid du gyur nas bstan pa 'byung bar 'gyur ba nyid/_/dpal ldan sdom pa chen po yon tan rgya mtsho'i 'byung gnas gsang ba mdzod 'di gsang ba'i don ston pa'o/_/de la 
[\[119b.3\]]brjod bya dang rjod byed du bcas pa'i rgyud kyi mtshan gyi rnam grangs bstan pa ni/_dpal he ru ka zhes bya ba sku gsung thugs rdo rje rnam thar sgo gsum skad cig ma rnam par dag pa'o/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po ni bla na med pa'i gsang chen no/_/nam mkha' dang mnyam pa 'bum pa'i nang nas byung ba zhes khyad 
[\[119b.4\]]par snying po lta bu nyid de rgya mtsho chen po las nor bu rin po che dbang gi rgyal po ltar ston pa dpal rdo rje dril bu pas gdan drangs pa'o/_/dngos grub kyi cho ga ni mchog dang thun mong gi grub pa myur du 'grub pa sbyin sreg dang 'khrul 'khor la sogs pa'i lam mo/_/shin tu rmad du byung ba ni byin 
[\[119b.5\]]rlabs phul du byung ba'o/_/bstan bcos thams cad pa ni 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i 'bras bu phun sum tshogs pa bstan pa'o/_/dpal he ru ka dpa' bo chen po ni bdud las rnam par rgyal ba bklags pas 'grub pa ni/_shin tu myur du don grub par byed 
[\[119b.6\]]pa'o/_/thams cad/_/mkhyen pa ni rang gzhan gnyis med rnam pa ma lus pa'i don to/_/gzhan gyis mi thub pa ni bar du gcod pa mi 'byung ba'o/_/gdod ma nas grub pa ni dpal he ru ka rdo rje 'chang gi dgongs pa'o/_/rgyud phyi ma ni dang po chen po la ltos pa'o/_/dpal 'khor lo sdom pa zhes bya ba 
[\[119b.7\]]rgyud sde rnams las 'di rnal 'byor chen po bla na med pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po las le'u lnga bcu rtsa gcig pa rdzogs so zhes yongs su bstan pa ma lus pa'i don to/_/dpal 'khor lo sdom pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po bde mchog bsdus pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par bshad pa in+d+ra b+hU ti'i zhal snga nas mdzad pa'o//_//
